# PyMOL

- **Category:** structbio
- **Papers in survey:** 700
- **Journals:** PNAS (458), Nature (178), Cell (53), Science (11)
- **Years:** 2021 (67), 2022 (141), 2023 (153), 2024 (135), 2025 (161), 2026 (43)
- **Versions named:** 2.5 (15), 2.5.2 (9), 2.5.4 (7), 2.4 (7), 2.5.0 (6), 2.4.0 (6), 2.4.1 (6), 3.1 (5), 2.0 (5), 2.6.0 (5)
- **Pipeline stages it appears in:** visualisation (319), alignment/mapping (49), structure determination (21), simulation/modelling (12), dimensionality reduction/clustering (11), differential/statistical testing (4), read trimming (1), normalisation (1), quantification (1)
